===== M0011 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1168-1241
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1168 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1169 |     switch (type) {
1170 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
1171 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
1172 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
1173 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
1174 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
1175 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
1176 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
1177 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
1178 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
1179 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1180 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1181 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1182 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
1183 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
1184 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
1185 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
1186 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
1187 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
1188 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
1189 |       case Token.EQ:
1190 |       case Token.NE:
1191 |       case Token.SHEQ:
1192 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
1193 |       case Token.LT:
1194 |       case Token.GT:
1195 |       case Token.LE:
1196 |       case Token.GE:
1197 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1198 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
1199 |       case Token.LSH:
1200 |       case Token.RSH:
1201 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
1202 |       case Token.SUB:
1203 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
1204 |       case Token.MUL:
1205 |       case Token.MOD:
1206 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
1207 |       case Token.INC:
1208 |       case Token.DEC:
1209 |       case Token.NEW:
1210 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1211 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
1212 |       case Token.VOID:
1213 |       case Token.NOT:
1214 |       case Token.BITNOT:
1215 |       case Token.POS:
1216 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
1217 | 
1218 |       case Token.CALL:
1219 |       case Token.GETELEM:
1220 |       case Token.GETPROP:
1221 |       // Data values
1222 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1223 |       case Token.EMPTY:  // TODO(johnlenz): remove this.
1224 |       case Token.FALSE:
1225 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1226 |       case Token.NAME:
1227 |       case Token.NULL:
1228 |       case Token.NUMBER:
1229 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
1230 |       case Token.REGEXP:
1231 |       case Token.STRING:
1232 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
1233 |       case Token.THIS:
1234 |       case Token.TRUE:
1235 |         return 15;
1236 | 
1237 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
1238 |                                Token.name(type) +
1239 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
1240 |     }
1241 |   }

===== M0022 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 490-506
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
490 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
491 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
492 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
493 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
494 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
495 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
496 |         String inputName = input.getName();
497 | 
498 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
499 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
500 |         // will get an error down the line.
501 |         inputs.add(input);
502 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
503 |       }
504 |     }
505 |     return inputs;
506 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * It isn't possible to always determine if a detached node is a expression,
   * so make a best guess.
   */
Method code:
499 |   private static boolean mayBeStatementNoReturn(Node n) {
500 |     switch (n.getType()) {
501 |       case Token.EMPTY:
502 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
503 |         // EMPTY and FUNCTION are used both in expression and statement
504 |         // contexts
505 |         return true;
506 | 
507 |       case Token.BLOCK:
508 |       case Token.BREAK:
509 |       case Token.CONST:
510 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
511 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
512 |       case Token.DO:
513 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
514 |       case Token.FOR:
515 |       case Token.IF:
516 |       case Token.LABEL:
517 |       case Token.SWITCH:
518 |       case Token.THROW:
519 |       case Token.TRY:
520 |       case Token.VAR:
521 |       case Token.WHILE:
522 |       case Token.WITH:
523 |         return true;
524 | 
525 |       default:
526 |         return false;
527 |     }
528 |   }

/**
   * Defines a property whose type is explicitly declared by the programmer.
   * @param propertyName the property's name
   * @param type the type
   * @param propertyNode the node corresponding to the declaration of property
   *        which might later be accessed using {@code getPropertyNode}.

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java

Documentation:

Method code:
192 |   private void tryReduceOperandsForOp(Node n) {
193 |     switch (n.getType()) {
194 |       case Token.ADD:
195 |         Node left = n.getFirstChild();
196 |         Node right = n.getLastChild();
197 |         if (!NodeUtil.mayBeString(left) && !NodeUtil.mayBeString(right)) {
198 |           tryConvertOperandsToNumber(n);
199 |         }
200 |         break;
201 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
202 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
203 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
204 |         // TODO(johnlenz): convert these to integers.
205 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
206 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
207 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
208 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
209 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
210 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
211 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
212 |         tryConvertToNumber(n.getLastChild());
213 |         break;
214 |       case Token.BITNOT:
215 |       case Token.BITOR:
216 |       case Token.BITXOR:
217 |       case Token.BITAND:
218 |       case Token.LSH:
219 |       case Token.RSH:
220 |       case Token.URSH:
221 |       case Token.SUB:
222 |       case Token.MUL:
223 |       case Token.MOD:
224 |       case Token.DIV:
225 |       case Token.POS:
226 |       case Token.NEG:
227 |         tryConvertOperandsToNumber(n);
228 |         break;
229 |     }
230 |   }

84 |     try {
85 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceFile.getName());
86 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceFile, sourceFile.getCode(),
87 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
88 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
89 |           logger_);
90 |     } catch (IOException e) {
91 |       compiler.report(
92 |           JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceFile.getName()));
93 |     }
94 |

96 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
97 |       root = IR.script();
98 |     } else {
99 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
103 |     // the source file and module.
104 |     root.setStaticSourceFile(sourceFile);
105 |   }

===== M0047 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 164-180
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
164 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
165 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.isCall());
166 | 
167 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
168 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
169 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
170 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
171 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
172 |       }
173 | 
174 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
175 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
176 |       if (first.isName() &&
177 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
178 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
179 |       }
180 |     }

Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

===== M0069 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeOptimizationsPass.traverse(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeOptimizationsPass.java
Original range: 113-138
Documentation:

Method code:
113 |   private void traverse(Node node) {
114 |     // The goal here is to avoid retraversing
115 |     // the entire AST to catch newly created opportunities.
116 |     // So we track whether a "unit of code" has changed,
117 |     // and revisit immediately.
118 |     if (!shouldVisit(node)) {
119 |       return;
120 |     }
121 | 
122 |     int visits = 0;
123 |     do {
124 |       Node c = node.getFirstChild();
125 |       while(c != null) {
126 |         Node next = c.getNext();
127 |         traverse(c);
128 |         c = next;
129 |       }
130 | 
131 |       visit(node);
132 |       visits++;
133 | 
134 |       Preconditions.checkState(visits < 10000, "too many interations");
135 |     } while (shouldRetraverse(node));
136 | 
137 |     exitNode(node);
138 |   }

===== M0076 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 504-524
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
504 |   @Override
505 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
506 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
507 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
508 |     if (unknown) {
509 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
510 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
511 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
512 |         unknown = false;
513 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
514 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
515 |             unknown = true;
516 |             break;
517 |           }
518 |         }
519 |       } else {
520 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
521 |       }
522 |     }
523 |     return unknown;
524 |   }

   * duplicate inputs.

518 |   void initInputsByIdMap() {
519 |     inputsById = new HashMap<InputId, CompilerInput>();

521 |       InputId id = input.getInputId();
522 |       CompilerInput previous = putCompilerInput(id, input);

===== M0087 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isBooleanResultHelper(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1369-1394
Documentation:

Method code:
1369 |   static boolean isBooleanResultHelper(Node n) {
1370 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1371 |       // Primitives
1372 |       case Token.TRUE:
1373 |       case Token.FALSE:
1374 |       // Comparisons
1375 |       case Token.EQ:
1376 |       case Token.NE:
1377 |       case Token.SHEQ:
1378 |       case Token.SHNE:
1379 |       case Token.LT:
1380 |       case Token.GT:
1381 |       case Token.LE:
1382 |       case Token.GE:
1383 |       // Queries
1384 |       case Token.IN:
1385 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1386 |       // Inversion
1387 |       case Token.NOT:
1388 |       // delete operator returns a boolean.
1389 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1390 |         return true;
1391 |       default:
1392 |         return false;
1393 |     }
1394 |   }

===== M0093 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.performArithmeticOp(int,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 701-776
Documentation:
/**
   * Try to fold arithmetic binary operators
   */
Method code:
701 |   private Node performArithmeticOp(int opType, Node left, Node right) {
702 |     // Unlike other operations, ADD operands are not always converted
703 |     // to Number.
704 |     if (opType == Token.ADD
705 |         && (NodeUtil.mayBeString(left, false)
706 |             || NodeUtil.mayBeString(right, false))) {
707 |       return null;
708 |     }
709 | 
710 |     double result;
711 | 
712 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Handle NaN with unknown value. BIT ops convert NaN
713 |     // to zero so this is a little awkward here.
714 | 
715 |     Double lValObj = NodeUtil.getNumberValue(left);
716 |     if (lValObj == null) {
717 |       return null;
718 |     }
719 |     Double rValObj = NodeUtil.getNumberValue(right);
720 |     if (rValObj == null) {
721 |       return null;
722 |     }
723 | 
724 |     double lval = lValObj;
725 |     double rval = rValObj;
726 | 
727 |     switch (opType) {
728 |       case Token.BITAND:
729 |         result = ScriptRuntime.toInt32(lval) & ScriptRuntime.toInt32(rval);
730 |         break;
731 |       case Token.BITOR:
732 |         result = ScriptRuntime.toInt32(lval) | ScriptRuntime.toInt32(rval);
733 |         break;
734 |       case Token.BITXOR:
735 |         result = ScriptRuntime.toInt32(lval) ^ ScriptRuntime.toInt32(rval);
736 |         break;
737 |       case Token.ADD:
738 |         result = lval + rval;
739 |         break;
740 |       case Token.SUB:
741 |         result = lval - rval;
742 |         break;
743 |       case Token.MUL:
744 |         result = lval * rval;
745 |         break;
746 |       case Token.MOD:
747 |         if (rval == 0) {
748 |           return null;
749 |         }
750 |         result = lval % rval;
751 |         break;
752 |       case Token.DIV:
753 |         if (rval == 0) {
754 |           return null;
755 |         }
756 |         result = lval / rval;
757 |         break;
758 |       default:
759 |         throw new Error("Unexpected arithmetic operator");
760 |     }
761 | 
762 |     // TODO(johnlenz): consider removing the result length check.
763 |     // length of the left and right value plus 1 byte for the operator.
764 |     if ((String.valueOf(result).length() <=
765 |         String.valueOf(lval).length() + String.valueOf(rval).length() + 1
766 | 
767 |         // Do not try to fold arithmetic for numbers > 2^53. After that
768 |         // point, fixed-point math starts to break down and become inaccurate.
769 |         && Math.abs(result) <= MAX_FOLD_NUMBER)
770 |         || Double.isNaN(result)
771 |         || result == Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY
772 |         || result == Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY) {
773 |       return NodeUtil.numberNode(result, null);
774 |     }
775 |     return null;
776 |   }

236 |     this.type = type;

240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 |

251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,

270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "

272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

===== M0123 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processStringLiteral(StringLiteral)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 917-944
Documentation:

Method code:
917 |     @Override
918 |     Node processStringLiteral(StringLiteral literalNode) {
919 |       String value = literalNode.getValue();
920 |       Node n = newStringNode(value);
921 |       if (value.indexOf('\u000B') != -1) {
922 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): In JavaScript, there are 3 ways to
923 |         // represent a vertical tab: \v, \x0B, \u000B.
924 |         // The \v notation was added later, and is not understood
925 |         // on IE. So we need to preserve it as-is. This is really
926 |         // obnoxious, because we do not have a good way to represent
927 |         // how the original string was encoded without making the
928 |         // representation of strings much more complicated.
929 |         //
930 |         // To handle this, we look at the original source test, and
931 |         // mark the string as \v-encoded or not. If a string is
932 |         // \v encoded, then all the vertical tabs in that string
933 |         // will be encoded with a \v.
934 |         int start = literalNode.getAbsolutePosition();
935 |         int end = start + literalNode.getLength();
936 |         if (start < sourceString.length() &&
937 |             (sourceString.substring(
938 |                  start, Math.min(sourceString.length(), end))
939 |              .indexOf("\\v") != -1)) {
940 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.SLASH_V, true);
941 |         }
942 |       }
943 |       return n;
944 |     }

===== M0124 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryFoldAdd(Node,Node,Node)

827 |   private Node tryFoldAdd(Node node, Node left, Node right) {
828 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(node.isAdd());
829 | 
830 |     if (NodeUtil.mayBeString(node, true)) {
831 |       if (NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(left, false) &&
832 |           NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(right, false)) {
833 |         // '6' + 7
834 |         return tryFoldAddConstantString(node, left, right);
835 |       } else {
836 |         // a + 7 or 6 + a
837 |         return tryFoldChildAddString(node, left, right);
838 |       }
839 |     } else {
840 |       // Try arithmetic add

842 |       if (result != node) {
843 |         return result;
844 |       }
845 |       return tryFoldLeftChildOp(node, left, right);
846 |     }

===== M0132 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getStringNumberValue(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 331-375
Documentation:

Method code:
331 |   static Double getStringNumberValue(String rawJsString) {
332 |     if (rawJsString.contains("\u000b")) {
333 |       // vertical tab is not always whitespace
334 |       return null;
335 |     }
336 | 
337 |     String s = trimJsWhiteSpace(rawJsString);
338 |     // return ScriptRuntime.toNumber(s);
339 |     if (s.length() == 0) {
340 |       return 0.0;
341 |     }
342 | 
343 |     if (s.length() > 2
344 |         && s.charAt(0) == '0'
345 |         && (s.charAt(1) == 'x' || s.charAt(1) == 'X')) {
346 |       // Attempt to convert hex numbers.
347 |       try {
348 |         return Double.valueOf(Integer.parseInt(s.substring(2), 16));
349 |       } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
350 |         return Double.NaN;
351 |       }
352 |     }
353 | 
354 |     if (s.length() > 3
355 |         && (s.charAt(0) == '-' || s.charAt(0) == '+')
356 |         && s.charAt(1) == '0'
357 |         && (s.charAt(2) == 'x' || s.charAt(2) == 'X')) {
358 |       // hex numbers with explicit signs vary between browsers.
359 |       return null;
360 |     }
361 | 
362 |     // Firefox and IE treat the "Infinity" differently. Firefox is case
363 |     // insensitive, but IE treats "infinity" as NaN.  So leave it alone.
364 |     if (s.equals("infinity")
365 |         || s.equals("-infinity")
366 |         || s.equals("+infinity")) {
367 |       return null;
368 |     }
369 | 
370 |     try {
371 |       return Double.parseDouble(s);
372 |     } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
373 |       return Double.NaN;
374 |     }
375 |   }

   * All passes should call reportCodeChange() when they alter
   * the JS tree structure. This is verified by CompilerTestCase.
   * This allows us to optimize to a fixed point.
   */
Method code:
1984 |   @Override
1985 |   public void reportCodeChange() {
1986 |     for (CodeChangeHandler handler : codeChangeHandlers) {
1987 |       handler.reportChange();
1988 |     }
1989 |   }

===== M0139 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryFoldBinaryOperator(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 99-179
Documentation:

Method code:
99 |   private Node tryFoldBinaryOperator(Node subtree) {
100 |     Node left = subtree.getFirstChild();
101 | 
102 |     if (left == null) {
103 |       return subtree;
104 |     }
105 | 
106 |     Node right = left.getNext();
107 | 
108 |     if (right == null) {
109 |       return subtree;
110 |     }
111 | 
112 |     // If we've reached here, node is truly a binary operator.
113 |     switch(subtree.getType()) {
114 |       case Token.GETPROP:
115 |         return tryFoldGetProp(subtree, left, right);
116 | 
117 |       case Token.GETELEM:
118 |         return tryFoldGetElem(subtree, left, right);
119 | 
120 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
121 |         return tryFoldInstanceof(subtree, left, right);
122 | 
123 |       case Token.AND:
124 |       case Token.OR:
125 |         return tryFoldAndOr(subtree, left, right);
126 | 
127 |       case Token.LSH:
128 |       case Token.RSH:
129 |       case Token.URSH:
130 |         return tryFoldShift(subtree, left, right);
131 | 
132 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
133 |         return tryFoldAssign(subtree, left, right);
134 | 
135 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
136 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
137 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
138 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
139 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
140 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
141 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
142 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
143 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
144 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
145 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
146 |         return tryUnfoldAssignOp(subtree, left, right);
147 | 
148 |       case Token.ADD:
149 |         return tryFoldAdd(subtree, left, right);
150 | 
151 |       case Token.SUB:
152 |       case Token.DIV:
153 |       case Token.MOD:
154 |         return tryFoldArithmeticOp(subtree, left, right);
155 | 
156 |       case Token.MUL:
157 |       case Token.BITAND:
158 |       case Token.BITOR:
159 |       case Token.BITXOR:
160 |         Node result = tryFoldArithmeticOp(subtree, left, right);
161 |         if (result != subtree) {
162 |           return result;
163 |         }
164 |         return tryFoldLeftChildOp(subtree, left, right);
165 | 
166 |       case Token.LT:
167 |       case Token.GT:
168 |       case Token.LE:
169 |       case Token.GE:
170 |       case Token.EQ:
171 |       case Token.NE:
172 |       case Token.SHEQ:
173 |       case Token.SHNE:
174 |         return tryFoldComparison(subtree, left, right);
175 | 
176 |       default:
177 |         return subtree;
178 |     }
179 |   }

===== M0145 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.hoistExterns(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1407-1444
Documentation:
/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @externs annotation into the externs list.
   */
Method code:
1407 |   private void hoistExterns(Node externsRoot) {
1408 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1409 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1410 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement() &&
1411 |           options.closurePass) {
1412 |         // If we're doing scanning dependency info anyway, use that
1413 |         // information to skip sources that obviously aren't externs.
1414 |         if (!input.getProvides().isEmpty() || !input.getRequires().isEmpty()) {
1415 |           continue;
1416 |         }
1417 |       }
1418 | 
1419 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1420 | 
1421 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1422 |       if (n == null) {
1423 |         continue;
1424 |       }
1425 | 
1426 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1427 |       if (info != null && info.isExterns()) {
1428 |         // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1429 |         // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1430 |         // the externs pile anyways.
1431 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1432 |         input.setIsExtern(true);
1433 | 
1434 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1435 | 
1436 |         externs.add(input);
1437 |         staleInputs = true;
1438 |       }
1439 |     }
1440 | 
1441 |     if (staleInputs) {
1442 |       repartitionInputs();
1443 |     }
1444 |   }

===== M0159 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AstValidator.validateChildCount(Node,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AstValidator.java
Original range: 773-787
Documentation:

Method code:
773 |   private void validateChildCount(Node n, int i) {
774 |     boolean valid = false;
775 |     if (i == 0) {
776 |       valid = !n.hasChildren();
777 |     } else if (i == 1) {
778 |       valid = n.hasOneChild();
779 |     } else {
780 |       valid = (n.getChildCount() == i);
781 |     }
782 |     if (!valid) {
783 |       violation(
784 |           "Expected " + i + " children, but was "
785 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);
786 |     }
787 |   }

===== M0163 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

===== M0174 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.allResultsMatch(Node,Predicate<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1261-1276
Documentation:
/**
   * Apply the supplied predicate against
   * all possible result Nodes of the expression.
   */
Method code:
1261 |   static boolean allResultsMatch(Node n, Predicate<Node> p) {
1262 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1263 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
1264 |       case Token.COMMA:
1265 |         return allResultsMatch(n.getLastChild(), p);
1266 |       case Token.AND:
1267 |       case Token.OR:
1268 |         return allResultsMatch(n.getFirstChild(), p)
1269 |             && allResultsMatch(n.getLastChild(), p);
1270 |       case Token.HOOK:
1271 |         return allResultsMatch(n.getFirstChild().getNext(), p)
1272 |             && allResultsMatch(n.getLastChild(), p);
1273 |       default:
1274 |         return p.apply(n);
1275 |     }
1276 |   }

===== M0176 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 121-214
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
121 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
122 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
123 |     // just bail out early here.
124 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
125 |       return this;
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
129 | 
130 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
131 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
132 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
133 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
134 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
135 |     }
136 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
137 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
138 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
139 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
140 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
141 |         }
142 |       } else {
143 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
144 |           return this;
145 |         }
146 | 
147 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
148 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
149 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
150 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
151 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
152 |           FunctionType other =
153 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
154 |           FunctionType supremum =
155 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
156 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
157 |           result = null;
158 |           return this;
159 |         }
160 | 
161 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
162 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
163 |         // one another.
164 |         int currentIndex = 0;
165 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
166 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType() ||
176 |               alternate.hasAnyTemplate() ||
177 |               current.hasAnyTemplate()) {
178 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
179 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
180 |               return this;
181 |             }
182 |           } else {
183 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
184 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
185 |               return this;
186 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
187 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
188 |               it.remove();
189 | 
190 |               if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
191 |                 functionTypePosition = -1;
192 |               } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
193 |                 functionTypePosition--;
194 |                 currentIndex--;
195 |               }
196 |             }
197 |           }
198 |           currentIndex++;
199 |         }
200 | 
201 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
202 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
203 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
204 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
205 |         }
206 | 
207 |         alternates.add(alternate);
208 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
209 |       }
210 |     } else {
211 |       result = null;
212 |     }
213 |     return this;
214 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ErrorFunctionType.ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry,String)

50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {

===== M0178 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 403-429
Documentation:

Method code:
403 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
404 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
405 |     // two cases:
406 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
407 |     // and
408 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
409 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
410 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
411 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
412 |     //
413 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
414 |     //
415 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
416 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
417 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
418 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
419 |     //
420 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
421 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
422 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
423 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
424 | 
425 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
426 |           registry, this.getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
427 |     }
428 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
429 |   }

===== M0180 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryConvertToNumber(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 240-275
Documentation:

Method code:
240 |   private void tryConvertToNumber(Node n) {
241 |     switch (n.getType()) {
242 |       case Token.NUMBER:
243 |         // Nothing to do
244 |         return;
245 |       case Token.AND:
246 |       case Token.OR:
247 |       case Token.COMMA:
248 |         tryConvertToNumber(n.getLastChild());
249 |         return;
250 |       case Token.HOOK:
251 |         tryConvertToNumber(n.getChildAtIndex(1));
252 |         tryConvertToNumber(n.getLastChild());
253 |         return;
254 |       case Token.NAME:
255 |         if (!NodeUtil.isUndefined(n)) {
256 |           return;
257 |         }
258 |         break;
259 |     }
260 | 
261 |     Double result = NodeUtil.getNumberValue(n);
262 |     if (result == null) {
263 |       return;
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     double value = result;
267 | 
268 |     Node replacement = NodeUtil.numberNode(value, n);
269 |     if (replacement.isEquivalentTo(n)) {
270 |       return;
271 |     }
272 | 
273 |     n.getParent().replaceChild(n, replacement);
274 |     reportCodeChange();
275 |   }

181 |   void add(String newcode) {
182 |     maybeEndStatement();
183 | 
184 |     if (newcode.length() == 0) {
185 |       return;
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     char c = newcode.charAt(0);
189 |     if ((isWordChar(c) || c == '\\') &&
190 |         isWordChar(getLastChar())) {

   * Copied from Rhino's ScriptRuntime

Method code:
394 |   public static TernaryValue isStrWhiteSpaceChar(int c) {
395 |     switch (c) {
396 |       case '\u000B': // <VT>
397 |         return TernaryValue.UNKNOWN;  // IE says "no", ECMAScript says "yes"
398 |       case ' ': // <SP>
399 |       case '\n': // <LF>
400 |       case '\r': // <CR>
401 |       case '\t': // <TAB>
402 |       case '\u00A0': // <NBSP>
403 |       case '\u000C': // <FF>
404 |       case '\u2028': // <LS>
405 |       case '\u2029': // <PS>
406 |       case '\uFEFF': // <BOM>
407 |         return TernaryValue.TRUE;
408 |       default:
409 |         return (Character.getType(c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR)
410 |             ? TernaryValue.TRUE : TernaryValue.FALSE;
411 |     }
412 |   }

===== M0200 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createParameters(boolean,JSType...)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1246-1257
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1246 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1247 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1248 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1249 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1250 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1251 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1252 |       } else {
1253 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1254 |       }
1255 |     }
1256 |     return builder.build();
1257 |   }

===== M0208 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 85-732
Documentation:

Method code:
85 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
86 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
87 |       return;
88 |     }
89 | 
90 |     int type = n.getType();
91 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
92 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
93 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
94 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
95 | 
96 |     // Handle all binary operators
97 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
98 |       Preconditions.checkState(
99 |           childCount == 2,
100 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
101 |           opstr, childCount);
102 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
103 | 
104 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
105 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
106 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
107 | 
108 |       // Handle associativity.
109 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
110 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
111 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
112 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
113 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
114 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
115 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
116 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
117 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
118 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
119 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
120 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
121 |       } else {
122 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, type, opstr, context, rhsContext, p, p + 1);
123 |       }
124 |       return;
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
128 | 
129 |     switch (type) {
130 |       case Token.TRY: {
131 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().isBlock() &&
132 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
133 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
134 | 
135 |         add("try");
136 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
137 | 
138 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
139 |         // isn't a catch block
140 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
141 |         if (catchblock != null) {
142 |           add(catchblock);
143 |         }
144 | 
145 |         if (childCount == 3) {
146 |           add("finally");
147 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
148 |         }
149 |         break;
150 |       }
151 | 
152 |       case Token.CATCH:
153 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
154 |         add("catch(");
155 |         add(first);
156 |         add(")");
157 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
158 |         break;
159 | 
160 |       case Token.THROW:
161 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
162 |         add("throw");
163 |         add(first);
164 | 
165 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
166 |         // parse this.
167 |         cc.endStatement(true);
168 |         break;
169 | 
170 |       case Token.RETURN:
171 |         add("return");
172 |         if (childCount == 1) {
173 |           add(first);
174 |         } else {
175 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
176 |         }
177 |         cc.endStatement();
178 |         break;
179 | 
180 |       case Token.VAR:
181 |         if (first != null) {
182 |           add("var ");
183 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
184 |         }
185 |         break;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
188 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
189 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
190 |         break;
191 | 
192 |       case Token.NAME:
193 |         if (first == null || first.isEmpty()) {
194 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
195 |         } else {
196 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
197 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
198 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
199 |           if (first.isComma()) {
200 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN), Context.OTHER);
201 |           } else {
202 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
203 |             // precedence.
204 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
205 |           }
206 |         }
207 |         break;
208 | 
209 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
210 |         add("[");
211 |         addArrayList(first);
212 |         add("]");
213 |         break;
214 | 
215 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
216 |         add("(");
217 |         addList(first);
218 |         add(")");
219 |         break;
220 | 
221 |       case Token.COMMA:
222 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
223 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, Token.COMMA, ",", context, Context.OTHER, 0, 0);
224 |         break;
225 | 
226 |       case Token.NUMBER:
227 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
228 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
229 |         break;
230 | 
231 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
232 |       case Token.VOID:
233 |       case Token.NOT:
234 |       case Token.BITNOT:
235 |       case Token.POS: {
236 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
237 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
238 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
239 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
240 |         break;
241 |       }
242 | 
243 |       case Token.NEG: {
244 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
245 | 
246 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
247 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
248 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
249 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isNumber()) {
250 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
251 |         } else {
252 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
253 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
254 |         }
255 | 
256 |         break;
257 |       }
258 | 
259 |       case Token.HOOK: {
260 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
261 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
262 |         addExpr(first, p + 1, context);
263 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
264 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1, Context.OTHER);
265 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
266 |         addExpr(last, 1, Context.OTHER);
267 |         break;
268 |       }
269 | 
270 |       case Token.REGEXP:
271 |         if (!first.isString() ||
272 |             !last.isString()) {
273 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
274 |         }
275 | 
276 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
277 | 
278 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
279 |         if (childCount == 2) {
280 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
281 |         } else {
282 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
283 |           add(regexp);
284 |         }
285 |         break;
286 | 
287 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
288 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
289 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
290 |         }
291 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
292 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
293 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
294 |           add("(");
295 |         }
296 | 
297 |         add("function");
298 |         add(first);
299 | 
300 |         add(first.getNext());
301 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
302 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
303 | 
304 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
305 |           add(")");
306 |         }
307 |         break;
308 | 
309 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
310 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
311 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().isObjectLit());
312 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
313 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.isFunction());
314 | 
315 |         // Get methods are unnamed
316 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
317 |         if (type == Token.GETTER_DEF) {
318 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
319 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
320 |           add("get ");
321 |         } else {
322 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
323 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
324 |           add("set ");
325 |         }
326 | 
327 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
328 |         String name = n.getString();
329 |         Node fn = first;
330 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
331 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
332 | 
333 |         // Add the property name.
334 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
335 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
336 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
337 |             // Unicode escaped.
338 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
339 |           add(name);
340 |         } else {
341 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
342 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
343 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
344 |             cc.addNumber(d);
345 |           } else {
346 |             addJsString(n);
347 |           }
348 |         }
349 | 
350 |         add(parameters);
351 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
352 |         break;
353 | 
354 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
355 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
356 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
357 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
358 |         }
359 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
360 |         if (preserveBlock) {
361 |           cc.beginBlock();
362 |         }
363 | 
364 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
365 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
366 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
367 |                 !preserveBlock &&
368 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
369 |                 n.getParent().isScript());
370 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
371 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
372 | 
373 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
374 |           if (c.isVar()) {
375 |             cc.endStatement();
376 |           }
377 | 
378 |           if (c.isFunction()) {
379 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
380 |           }
381 | 
382 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
383 |           // because top-level statements are more homogeneous.
384 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
385 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
386 |           }
387 |         }
388 |         if (preserveBlock) {
389 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
390 |         }
391 |         break;
392 |       }
393 | 
394 |       case Token.FOR:
395 |         if (childCount == 4) {
396 |           add("for(");
397 |           if (first.isVar()) {
398 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
399 |           } else {
400 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
401 |           }
402 |           add(";");
403 |           add(first.getNext());
404 |           add(";");
405 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
406 |           add(")");
407 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
408 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
409 |         } else {
410 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
411 |           add("for(");
412 |           add(first);
413 |           add("in");
414 |           add(first.getNext());
415 |           add(")");
416 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
417 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
418 |         }
419 |         break;
420 | 
421 |       case Token.DO:
422 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
423 |         add("do");
424 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
425 |         add("while(");
426 |         add(last);
427 |         add(")");
428 |         cc.endStatement();
429 |         break;
430 | 
431 |       case Token.WHILE:
432 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
433 |         add("while(");
434 |         add(first);
435 |         add(")");
436 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
437 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
438 |         break;
439 | 
440 |       case Token.EMPTY:
441 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
442 |         break;
443 | 
444 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
445 |         Preconditions.checkState(
446 |             childCount == 2,
447 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
448 |         Preconditions.checkState(
449 |             last.isString(),
450 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
451 |         boolean needsParens = (first.isNumber());
452 |         if (needsParens) {
453 |           add("(");
454 |         }
455 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
456 |         if (needsParens) {
457 |           add(")");
458 |         }
459 |         add(".");
460 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
461 |         break;
462 |       }
463 | 
464 |       case Token.GETELEM:
465 |         Preconditions.checkState(
466 |             childCount == 2,
467 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
468 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
469 |         add("[");
470 |         add(first.getNext());
471 |         add("]");
472 |         break;
473 | 
474 |       case Token.WITH:
475 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
476 |         add("with(");
477 |         add(first);
478 |         add(")");
479 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
480 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
481 |         break;
482 | 
483 |       case Token.INC:
484 |       case Token.DEC: {
485 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
486 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
487 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
488 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
489 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
490 |         if (postProp != 0) {
491 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
492 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
493 |         } else {
494 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
495 |           add(first);
496 |         }
497 |         break;
498 |       }
499 | 
500 |       case Token.CALL:
501 |         // We have two special cases here:
502 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
503 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
504 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
505 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
506 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
507 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
508 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
509 |         // that must be preserved.
510 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
511 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
512 |           add("(0,");
513 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA), Context.OTHER);
514 |           add(")");
515 |         } else {
516 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
517 |         }
518 |         add("(");
519 |         addList(first.getNext());
520 |         add(")");
521 |         break;
522 | 
523 |       case Token.IF:
524 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
525 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
526 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
527 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
528 |           cc.beginBlock();
529 |         }
530 | 
531 |         add("if(");
532 |         add(first);
533 |         add(")");
534 | 
535 |         if (hasElse) {
536 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
537 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
538 |           add("else");
539 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
540 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
541 |         } else {
542 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
543 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
544 |         }
545 | 
546 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
547 |           cc.endBlock();
548 |         }
549 |         break;
550 | 
551 |       case Token.NULL:
552 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
553 |         cc.addConstant("null");
554 |         break;
555 | 
556 |       case Token.THIS:
557 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
558 |         add("this");
559 |         break;
560 | 
561 |       case Token.FALSE:
562 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
563 |         cc.addConstant("false");
564 |         break;
565 | 
566 |       case Token.TRUE:
567 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
568 |         cc.addConstant("true");
569 |         break;
570 | 
571 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
572 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
573 |         add("continue");
574 |         if (childCount == 1) {
575 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
576 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
577 |           }
578 |           add(" ");
579 |           add(first);
580 |         }
581 |         cc.endStatement();
582 |         break;
583 | 
584 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
585 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
586 |         add("debugger");
587 |         cc.endStatement();
588 |         break;
589 | 
590 |       case Token.BREAK:
591 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
592 |         add("break");
593 |         if (childCount == 1) {
594 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
595 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
596 |           }
597 |           add(" ");
598 |           add(first);
599 |         }
600 |         cc.endStatement();
601 |         break;
602 | 
603 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
604 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
605 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
606 |         cc.endStatement();
607 |         break;
608 | 
609 |       case Token.NEW:
610 |         add("new ");
611 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
612 | 
613 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
614 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
615 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
616 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(
617 |             first, Token.CALL, NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION)) {
618 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
619 |         }
620 |         addExpr(first, precedence, Context.OTHER);
621 | 
622 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
623 |         Node next = first.getNext();
624 |         if (next != null) {
625 |           add("(");
626 |           addList(next);
627 |           add(")");
628 |         }
629 |         break;
630 | 
631 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
632 |         Preconditions.checkState(
633 |             childCount == 1, "Object lit key must have 1 child");
634 |         addJsString(n);
635 |         break;
636 | 
637 |       case Token.STRING:
638 |         Preconditions.checkState(
639 |             childCount == 0, "A string may not have children");
640 |         addJsString(n);
641 |         break;
642 | 
643 |       case Token.DELPROP:
644 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
645 |         add("delete ");
646 |         add(first);
647 |         break;
648 | 
649 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
650 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
651 |         if (needsParens) {
652 |           add("(");
653 |         }
654 |         add("{");
655 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
656 |           if (c != first) {
657 |             cc.listSeparator();
658 |           }
659 | 
660 |           if (c.isGetterDef() || c.isSetterDef()) {
661 |             add(c);
662 |           } else {
663 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.isStringKey());
664 |             String key = c.getString();
665 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
666 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
667 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
668 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
669 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
670 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
671 |                 // were Unicode escaped.
672 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
673 |               add(key);
674 |             } else {
675 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
676 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
677 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
678 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
679 |               } else {
680 |                 addExpr(c, 1, Context.OTHER);
681 |               }
682 |             }
683 |             add(":");
684 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1, Context.OTHER);
685 |           }
686 |         }
687 |         add("}");
688 |         if (needsParens) {
689 |           add(")");
690 |         }
691 |         break;
692 |       }
693 | 
694 |       case Token.SWITCH:
695 |         add("switch(");
696 |         add(first);
697 |         add(")");
698 |         cc.beginBlock();
699 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
700 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
701 |         break;
702 | 
703 |       case Token.CASE:
704 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
705 |         add("case ");
706 |         add(first);
707 |         addCaseBody(last);
708 |         break;
709 | 
710 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
711 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
712 |         add("default");
713 |         addCaseBody(first);
714 |         break;
715 | 
716 |       case Token.LABEL:
717 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
718 |         if (!first.isLabelName()) {
719 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
720 |         }
721 |         add(first);
722 |         add(":");
723 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
724 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
725 |         break;
726 | 
727 |       default:
728 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
729 |     }
730 | 
731 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
732 |   }

===== M0211 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 619-631
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
619 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
620 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
621 |     if (typeSet == null) {
622 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
623 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
624 |     }
625 | 
626 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
627 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
628 | 
629 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
630 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
631 |   }

===== M0213 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeOptimizationsPass.shouldRetraverse(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeOptimizationsPass.java
Original range: 140-154
Documentation:

Method code:
140 |   private boolean shouldRetraverse(Node node) {
141 |     if (node.getParent() != null && node.isFunction() || node.isScript()) {
142 |       ScopeState state = traversalState.peek();
143 |       if (state.changed) {
144 |         // prepare to re-visit the scope:
145 |         // when revisiting, only visit the immediate scope
146 |         // this reduces the cost of getting to a fixed
147 |         // point in global scope.
148 |         state.changed = false;
149 |         state.traverseChildScopes = false;
150 |         return true;
151 |       }
152 |     }
153 |     return false;
154 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeCutLine()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java

Documentation:
/**
     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.
     */
Method code:
489 |     @Override
490 |     void maybeCutLine() {
491 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
492 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
493 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
494 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
495 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
496 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
497 |           prevCutPosition = position;
498 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition, true);
499 |           lineIndex++;
500 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
501 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
502 |         } else {
503 |           startNewLine();
504 |         }
505 |       }
506 |     }

===== M0229 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.unrollBinaryOperator(Node,int,String,Context,Context,int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 741-757
Documentation:
/**
   * We could use addList recursively here, but sometimes we produce
   * very deeply nested operators and run out of stack space, so we
   * just unroll the recursion when possible.
   *
   * We assume nodes are left-recursive.
   */
Method code:
741 |   private void unrollBinaryOperator(
742 |       Node n, int op, String opStr, Context context,
743 |       Context rhsContext, int leftPrecedence, int rightPrecedence) {
744 |     Node firstNonOperator = n.getFirstChild();
745 |     while (firstNonOperator.getType() == op) {
746 |       firstNonOperator = firstNonOperator.getFirstChild();
747 |     }
748 | 
749 |     addExpr(firstNonOperator, leftPrecedence, context);
750 | 
751 |     Node current = firstNonOperator;
752 |     do {
753 |       current = current.getParent();
754 |       cc.addOp(opStr, true);
755 |       addExpr(current.getFirstChild().getNext(), rightPrecedence, rhsContext);
756 |     } while (current != n);
757 |   }

===== M0251 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 477-506
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
477 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
478 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
479 |     int type = n.getType();
480 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
481 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
482 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
483 |     }
484 | 
485 |     curNode = n;
486 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
487 | 
488 |     switch (type) {
489 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
490 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
491 |         break;
492 | 
493 |       default:
494 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
495 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
496 |           // would no longer point to the true next
497 |           Node next = child.getNext();
498 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
499 |           child = next;
500 |         }
501 |         break;
502 |     }
503 | 
504 |     curNode = n;
505 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
506 |   }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *

===== M0269 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 240-276
Documentation:

Method code:
240 |   void addNumber(double x) {
241 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
242 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
243 |     char prev = getLastChar();
244 |     boolean negativeZero = isNegativeZero(x);
245 |     if ((x < 0 || negativeZero) && prev == '-') {
246 |       add(" ");
247 |     }
248 | 
249 |     if (negativeZero) {
250 |       addConstant("-0");
251 |     } else if ((long) x == x) {
252 |       long value = (long) x;
253 |       long mantissa = value;
254 |       int exp = 0;
255 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
256 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
257 |           mantissa /= 10;
258 |           exp++;
259 |         }
260 |       }
261 |       if (exp > 2) {
262 |         addConstant(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
263 |       } else {
264 |         long valueAbs = Math.abs(value);
265 |         if (Long.toHexString(valueAbs).length() + 2 <
266 |             Long.toString(valueAbs).length()) {
267 |           addConstant((value < 0 ? "-" : "") + "0x" +
268 |               Long.toHexString(valueAbs));
269 |         } else {
270 |           addConstant(Long.toString(value));
271 |         }
272 |       }
273 |     } else {
274 |       addConstant(String.valueOf(x).replace(".0E", "E"));
275 |     }
276 |   }

1033 |     for (int i = 0; i != level; ++i) {
1034 |       sb.append("    ");
1035 |     }
1036 |     sb.append(n.toString());
1037 |     sb.append('\n');
1038 |     for (Node cursor = n.getFirstChild();
1039 |          cursor != null;
1040 |          cursor = cursor.getNext()) {
1041 |       toStringTreeHelper(cursor, level + 1, sb);
1042 |     }
1043 |   }

===== M0304 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.trimJsWhiteSpace(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 377-389
Documentation:

Method code:
377 |   static String trimJsWhiteSpace(String s) {
378 |     int start = 0;
379 |     int end = s.length();
380 |     while (end > 0
381 |         && isStrWhiteSpaceChar(s.charAt(end - 1)) == TernaryValue.TRUE) {
382 |       end--;
383 |     }
384 |     while (start < end
385 |         && isStrWhiteSpaceChar(s.charAt(start)) == TernaryValue.TRUE) {
386 |       start++;
387 |     }
388 |     return s.substring(start, end);
389 |   }

===== M0308 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 104-147
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 | 
117 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
118 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
119 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
120 | 
121 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
123 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
125 | 
126 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
127 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
128 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
129 | 
130 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
131 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
132 |     try {
133 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
134 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
135 |       logger.info(
136 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
137 |     } finally {
138 |       Context.exit();
139 |     }
140 |     Node root = null;
141 |     if (astRoot != null) {
142 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
143 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
144 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
145 |     }
146 |     return root;
147 |   }

===== M0322 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1280-1402
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1280 |   Node parseInputs() {
1281 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1282 | 
1283 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1284 |     // individual file parse trees.
1285 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1286 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1287 |     }
1288 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1289 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1290 |     }
1291 | 
1292 |     // Parse main JS sources.
1293 |     jsRoot = IR.block();
1294 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1295 | 
1296 |     externsRoot = IR.block();
1297 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1298 | 
1299 |     externAndJsRoot = IR.block(externsRoot, jsRoot);
1300 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1301 | 
1302 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1303 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot, options.tracer);
1304 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1305 |     }
1306 | 
1307 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1308 | 
1309 |     try {
1310 |       // Parse externs sources.
1311 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1312 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1313 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1314 |           return null;
1315 |         }
1316 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1317 |       }
1318 | 
1319 |       // Modules inferred in ProcessCommonJS pass.
1320 |       if (options.transformAMDToCJSModules || options.processCommonJSModules) {
1321 |         processAMDAndCommonJSModules();
1322 |       }
1323 | 
1324 |       hoistExterns(externsRoot);
1325 | 
1326 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1327 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1328 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1329 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1330 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1331 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1332 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1333 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1334 |           }
1335 |         }
1336 | 
1337 |         try {
1338 |           inputs =
1339 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1340 |               .manageDependencies(options.dependencyOptions, inputs);
1341 |           staleInputs = true;
1342 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1343 |           report(JSError.make(
1344 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1345 | 
1346 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1347 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1348 |             return null;
1349 |           }
1350 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1351 |           report(JSError.make(
1352 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1353 | 
1354 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1355 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1356 |             return null;
1357 |           }
1358 |         }
1359 |       }
1360 | 
1361 |       hoistNoCompileFiles();
1362 | 
1363 |       if (staleInputs) {
1364 |         repartitionInputs();
1365 |       }
1366 | 
1367 |       // Build the AST.
1368 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1369 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1370 |         if (n == null) {
1371 |           continue;
1372 |         }
1373 | 
1374 |         if (devMode) {
1375 |           runSanityCheck();
1376 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1377 |             return null;
1378 |           }
1379 |         }
1380 | 
1381 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1382 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1383 | 
1384 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1385 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1386 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1387 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1388 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1389 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1390 |         }
1391 | 
1392 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1393 |       }
1394 | 
1395 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1396 |         return null;
1397 |       }
1398 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1399 |     } finally {
1400 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1401 |     }
1402 |   }
