===== M0325 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 443-490
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |   @Override
444 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
445 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
446 |       return true;
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // Union types
450 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
451 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
452 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
453 |       return false;
454 |     }
455 | 
456 |     // record types
457 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
458 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     // Interfaces
462 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
463 |     // to the interface instance.
464 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
465 |     FunctionType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
466 | 
467 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
468 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
469 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
470 |           return true;
471 |         }
472 |       }
473 |     } else if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
474 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
475 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
476 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
477 |           return true;
478 |         }
479 |       }
480 |     }
481 | 
482 |     // other prototype based objects
483 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
484 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
485 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
486 |       // to avoid guessing.
487 |       return true;
488 |     }
489 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
490 |   }

===== M0354 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1198-1206
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1198 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1199 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1200 |       return -1;
1201 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1202 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1203 |     } else {
1204 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1205 |     }
1206 |   }

/**
   * Returns true if the operator is associative.
   * e.g. (a * b) * c = a * (b * c)
   * Note: "+" is not associative because it is also the concatenation
   * for strings. e.g. "a" + (1 + 2) is not "a" + 1 + 2
   */

1440 |       case Token.BITXOR:

1442 |         return true;
1443 |       default:
1444 |         return false;
1445 |     }
1446 |   }

===== M0375 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getNumberValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 272-329
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the value of a node as a Number, or null if it cannot be converted.
   * When it returns a non-null Double, this method effectively emulates the
   * <code>Number()</code> JavaScript cast function.
   */
Method code:
272 |   static Double getNumberValue(Node n) {
273 |     switch (n.getType()) {
274 |       case Token.TRUE:
275 |         return 1.0;
276 | 
277 |       case Token.FALSE:
278 |       case Token.NULL:
279 |         return 0.0;
280 | 
281 |       case Token.NUMBER:
282 |         return n.getDouble();
283 | 
284 |       case Token.VOID:
285 |         if (mayHaveSideEffects(n.getFirstChild())) {
286 |           return null;
287 |         } else {
288 |           return Double.NaN;
289 |         }
290 | 
291 |       case Token.NAME:
292 |         // Check for known constants
293 |         String name = n.getString();
294 |         if (name.equals("undefined")) {
295 |           return Double.NaN;
296 |         }
297 |         if (name.equals("NaN")) {
298 |           return Double.NaN;
299 |         }
300 |         if (name.equals("Infinity")) {
301 |           return Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
302 |         }
303 |         return null;
304 | 
305 |       case Token.NEG:
306 |         if (n.getChildCount() == 1 && n.getFirstChild().isName()
307 |             && n.getFirstChild().getString().equals("Infinity")) {
308 |           return Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
309 |         }
310 |         return null;
311 | 
312 |       case Token.NOT:
313 |         TernaryValue child = getPureBooleanValue(n.getFirstChild());
314 |         if (child != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
315 |           return child.toBoolean(true) ? 0.0 : 1.0; // reversed.
316 |         }
317 |         break;
318 | 
319 |       case Token.STRING:
320 |         return getStringNumberValue(n.getString());
321 | 
322 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
323 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
324 |         String value = getStringValue(n);
325 |         return value != null ? getStringNumberValue(value) : null;
326 |     }
327 | 
328 |     return null;
329 |   }

===== M0380 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 221-242
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
221 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
222 |     if (isAllType) {
223 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
224 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
225 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
226 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
227 |       } else {
228 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
229 |       }
230 |     } else {
231 |       int size = alternates.size();
232 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
233 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
234 |       } else if (size > 1) {
235 |         return null;
236 |       } else if (size == 1) {
237 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
238 |       } else {
239 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
240 |       }
241 |     }
242 |   }

Method code:
120 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
121 |     switch (node.getType()) {
122 |       case Token.ADD:
123 |       case Token.AND:

126 |       case Token.BITXOR:

132 |       case Token.IN:
133 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
134 |       case Token.LE:
135 |       case Token.LSH:
136 |       case Token.LT:
137 |       case Token.MOD:
138 |       case Token.MUL:
139 |       case Token.NE:
140 |       case Token.OR:
141 |       case Token.RSH:
142 |       case Token.SHEQ:
143 |       case Token.SHNE:
144 |       case Token.SUB:
145 |       case Token.URSH:
146 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
147 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
148 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
159 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
160 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
161 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
162 |       case Token.BITNOT:
163 |       case Token.DEC:
164 |       case Token.DELPROP:
165 |       case Token.INC:
166 |       case Token.NEG:
167 |       case Token.NOT:
168 |       case Token.POS:
169 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
170 |       case Token.VOID:
171 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
172 |       case Token.BLOCK:
173 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
174 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
175 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
176 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
177 |         } else {
178 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
179 |                                           node.getClass() +
180 |                                           " type: " +
181 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
182 |         }
183 |       case Token.BREAK:
184 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
185 |       case Token.CALL:
186 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
187 |       case Token.CASE:
188 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
189 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
190 |       case Token.CATCH:
191 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
192 |       case Token.COLON:
193 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
194 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
195 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
196 |       case Token.DO:
197 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
198 |       case Token.EMPTY:
199 |         return (node instanceof EmptyExpression) ?
200 |             processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node) :
201 |             processEmptyStatement((EmptyStatement) node);
202 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
203 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
204 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
205 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
206 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
207 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
208 |         } else {
209 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
210 |                                           node.getClass() +
211 |                                           " type: " +
212 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
213 |         }
214 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
215 |       case Token.FALSE:
216 |       case Token.NULL:
217 |       case Token.THIS:
218 |       case Token.TRUE:
219 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
220 |       case Token.FOR:
221 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
222 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
223 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
224 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
225 |         } else {
226 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
227 |                                           node.getClass() +
228 |                                           " type: " +
229 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
230 |         }
231 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
232 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
233 |       case Token.GETELEM:
234 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
235 |       case Token.GETPROP:
236 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
237 |       case Token.HOOK:
238 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
239 |       case Token.IF:
240 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
241 |       case Token.LABEL:
242 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
243 |       case Token.LP:
244 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
245 |       case Token.NAME:
246 |         return processName((Name) node);
247 |       case Token.NEW:
248 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
249 |       case Token.NUMBER:
250 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
251 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
252 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
253 |       case Token.REGEXP:
254 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
255 |       case Token.RETURN:
256 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
257 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
258 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
259 |       case Token.STRING:
260 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
261 |       case Token.SWITCH:
262 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
263 |       case Token.THROW:
264 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
265 |       case Token.TRY:
266 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
267 |       case Token.CONST:
268 |       case Token.VAR:
269 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
270 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
271 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
272 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
273 |         } else {
274 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
275 |                                           node.getClass() +
276 |                                           " type: " +
277 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
278 |         }
279 |       case Token.WHILE:
280 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
281 |       case Token.WITH:
282 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
283 |     }
284 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
285 |   }

Original range: 951-1010

Method code:
951 |   private void toString(
952 |       StringBuilder sb,
953 |       boolean printSource,
954 |       boolean printAnnotations,
955 |       boolean printType) {
956 |     sb.append(Token.name(type));
957 |     if (this instanceof StringNode) {
958 |       sb.append(' ');
959 |       sb.append(getString());
960 |     } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
961 |       sb.append(' ');
962 |       // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
963 |       // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
964 |       // toStringTree.
965 |       if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
966 |         sb.append("<invalid>");
967 |       } else {
968 |         sb.append(first.getString());
969 |       }
970 |     } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
971 |       sb.append(' ');
972 |       sb.append(getDouble());
973 |     }
974 |     if (printSource) {
975 |       int lineno = getLineno();
976 |       if (lineno != -1) {
977 |         sb.append(' ');
978 |         sb.append(lineno);
979 |       }
980 |     }
981 | 
982 |     if (printAnnotations) {
983 |       int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
984 |       for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
985 |         int type = keys[i];
986 |         PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
987 |         sb.append(" [");
988 |         sb.append(propToString(type));
989 |         sb.append(": ");
990 |         String value;
991 |         switch (type) {
992 |           default:
993 |             value = x.toString();
994 |             break;
995 |         }
996 |         sb.append(value);
997 |         sb.append(']');
998 |       }
999 |     }
1000 | 
1001 |     if (printType) {
1002 |       if (jsType != null) {
1003 |         String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
1004 |         if (jsTypeString != null) {
1005 |           sb.append(" : ");
1006 |           sb.append(jsTypeString);
1007 |         }
1008 |       }
1009 |     }
1010 |   }

===== M0388 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1511-1560
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1511 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1512 |     if (type != node.getType()
1513 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1514 |         || this.getClass() != node.getClass()) {
1515 |       return false;
1516 |     }
1517 | 
1518 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1519 |       return false;
1520 |     }
1521 | 
1522 |     if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1523 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1524 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1525 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1526 |         return false;
1527 |       }
1528 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING || type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1529 |       if (type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1530 |         int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1531 |         int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1532 |         if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1533 |           return false;
1534 |         }
1535 |       }
1536 | 
1537 |       int slashV1 = this.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1538 |       int slashV2 = node.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1539 |       if (slashV1 != slashV2) {
1540 |         return false;
1541 |       }
1542 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1543 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1544 |         return false;
1545 |       }
1546 |     }
1547 | 
1548 |     if (recurse) {
1549 |       Node n, n2;
1550 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1551 |            n != null;
1552 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1553 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1554 |           return false;
1555 |         }
1556 |       }
1557 |     }
1558 | 
1559 |     return true;
1560 |   }

===== M0398 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryFoldGetProp(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 1372-1407
Documentation:
/**
   * Try to fold array-length. e.g [1, 2, 3].length ==> 3, [x, y].length ==> 2
   */
Method code:
1372 |   private Node tryFoldGetProp(Node n, Node left, Node right) {
1373 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(n.isGetProp());
1374 | 
1375 |     if (left.isObjectLit()) {
1376 |       return tryFoldObjectPropAccess(n, left, right);
1377 |     }
1378 | 
1379 |     if (right.isString() &&
1380 |         right.getString().equals("length")) {
1381 |       int knownLength = -1;
1382 |       switch (left.getType()) {
1383 |         case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1384 |           if (mayHaveSideEffects(left)) {
1385 |             // Nope, can't fold this, without handling the side-effects.
1386 |             return n;
1387 |           }
1388 |           knownLength = left.getChildCount();
1389 |           break;
1390 |         case Token.STRING:
1391 |           knownLength = left.getString().length();
1392 |           break;
1393 |         default:
1394 |           // Not a foldable case, forget it.
1395 |           return n;
1396 |       }
1397 | 
1398 |       Preconditions.checkState(knownLength != -1);
1399 |       Node lengthNode = IR.number(knownLength);
1400 |       n.getParent().replaceChild(n, lengthNode);
1401 |       reportCodeChange();
1402 | 
1403 |       return lengthNode;
1404 |     }
1405 | 
1406 |     return n;
1407 |   }

633 |   private void addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(

643 |       typeSet.put(objType.getReferenceName(), objType);
644 |     } else if (type instanceof NamedType) {
645 |       addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
646 |           propertyName, ((NamedType) type).getReferencedType());
647 |     } else if (type.isUnionType()) {
648 |       for (JSType alternate : type.toMaybeUnionType().getAlternates()) {
649 |         addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, alternate);
650 |       }
651 |     }
652 |   }

===== M0424 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 470-485
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
470 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
471 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
472 |       Set<String> directives = null;
473 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
474 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
475 |         if (directives == null) {
476 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
477 |         } else {
478 |           directives.add(directive);
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 | 
482 |       if (directives != null) {
483 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
484 |       }
485 |     }

===== M0443 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 494-517
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is an immutable value.
   */
Method code:
494 |   static boolean isImmutableValue(Node n) {
495 |     switch (n.getType()) {
496 |       case Token.STRING:
497 |       case Token.NUMBER:
498 |       case Token.NULL:
499 |       case Token.TRUE:
500 |       case Token.FALSE:
501 |         return true;
502 |       case Token.NOT:
503 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
504 |       case Token.VOID:
505 |       case Token.NEG:
506 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
507 |       case Token.NAME:
508 |         String name = n.getString();
509 |         // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the keyword
510 |         // undefined to something other than the value undefined.
511 |         return "undefined".equals(name)
512 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
513 |             || "NaN".equals(name);
514 |     }
515 | 
516 |     return false;
517 |   }

===== M0452 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1081-1083
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
1081 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1082 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
1083 |   }

===== M0454 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 797-810
Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
797 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
798 |     if (item == null) {
799 |       return null;
800 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
801 |       return item.getNext();
802 |     } else {
803 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
804 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
805 |         return item.chain(result);
806 |       } else {
807 |         return item;
808 |       }
809 |     }
810 |   }

/**
   * Gets the {@code prototype} property of this function type. This is
   * equivalent to {@code (ObjectType) getPropertyType("prototype")}.
   */
Method code:
370 |   public ObjectType getPrototype() {
371 |     // lazy initialization of the prototype field
372 |     if (prototypeSlot == null) {

374 |       if (refName == null) {
375 |         // Someone is trying to access the prototype of a structural function.

389 |       }
390 |     }

392 |   }

===== M0478 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryFoldComparison(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 919-1150
Documentation:
/**
   * Try to fold comparison nodes, e.g ==
   */
Method code:
919 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
920 |   private Node tryFoldComparison(Node n, Node left, Node right) {
921 |     boolean leftLiteral = NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(left, true);
922 |     boolean rightLiteral = NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(right, true);
923 | 
924 |     if (!leftLiteral || !rightLiteral) {
925 |       // We only handle literal operands for LT and GT.
926 |       if (n.getType() != Token.GT && n.getType() != Token.LT) {
927 |         return n;
928 |       }
929 |     }
930 | 
931 |     int op = n.getType();
932 |     boolean result;
933 | 
934 |     boolean undefinedRight = NodeUtil.isUndefined(right) && rightLiteral;
935 |     boolean nullRight = right.isNull();
936 |     int lhType = getNormalizedNodeType(left);
937 |     int rhType = getNormalizedNodeType(right);
938 |     switch (lhType) {
939 |       case Token.VOID:
940 |         if (!leftLiteral) {
941 |           return n;
942 |         } else if (!rightLiteral) {
943 |           return n;
944 |         } else {
945 |           result = compareToUndefined(right, op);
946 |         }
947 |         break;
948 |       case Token.NULL:
949 |         if (rightLiteral && isEqualityOp(op)) {
950 |           result = compareToNull(right, op);
951 |           break;
952 |         }
953 |         // fallthrough
954 |       case Token.TRUE:
955 |       case Token.FALSE:
956 |         if (undefinedRight) {
957 |           result = compareToUndefined(left, op);
958 |           break;
959 |         }
960 |         if (rhType != Token.TRUE &&
961 |             rhType != Token.FALSE &&
962 |             rhType != Token.NULL) {
963 |           return n;
964 |         }
965 |         switch (op) {
966 |           case Token.SHEQ:
967 |           case Token.EQ:
968 |             result = lhType == rhType;
969 |             break;
970 | 
971 |           case Token.SHNE:
972 |           case Token.NE:
973 |             result = lhType != rhType;
974 |             break;
975 | 
976 |           case Token.GE:
977 |           case Token.LE:
978 |           case Token.GT:
979 |           case Token.LT:
980 |             Boolean compareResult = compareAsNumbers(op, left, right);
981 |             if (compareResult != null) {
982 |               result = compareResult;
983 |             } else {
984 |               return n;
985 |             }
986 |             break;
987 | 
988 |           default:
989 |             return n;  // we only handle == and != here
990 |         }
991 |         break;
992 | 
993 |       case Token.THIS:
994 |         if (!right.isThis()) {
995 |           return n;
996 |         }
997 |         switch (op) {
998 |           case Token.SHEQ:
999 |           case Token.EQ:
1000 |             result = true;
1001 |             break;
1002 | 
1003 |           case Token.SHNE:
1004 |           case Token.NE:
1005 |             result = false;
1006 |             break;
1007 | 
1008 |           // We can only handle == and != here.
1009 |           // GT, LT, GE, LE depend on the type of "this" and how it will
1010 |           // be converted to number.  The results are different depending on
1011 |           // whether it is a string, NaN or other number value.
1012 |           default:
1013 |             return n;
1014 |         }
1015 |         break;
1016 | 
1017 |       case Token.STRING:
1018 |         if (undefinedRight) {
1019 |           result = compareToUndefined(left, op);
1020 |           break;
1021 |         }
1022 |         if (nullRight && isEqualityOp(op)) {
1023 |           result = compareToNull(left, op);
1024 |           break;
1025 |         }
1026 |         if (Token.STRING != right.getType()) {
1027 |           return n;  // Only eval if they are the same type
1028 |         }
1029 | 
1030 |         TernaryValue ternary = TernaryValue.UNKNOWN;
1031 |         switch (op) {
1032 |           case Token.SHEQ:
1033 |           case Token.EQ:
1034 |             ternary = areStringsEqual(left.getString(), right.getString());
1035 |             break;
1036 | 
1037 |           case Token.SHNE:
1038 |           case Token.NE:
1039 |             ternary =
1040 |                 areStringsEqual(left.getString(), right.getString()).not();
1041 |             break;
1042 |         }
1043 | 
1044 |         if (ternary == TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
1045 |           return n;
1046 |         }
1047 |         result = ternary.toBoolean(true);
1048 |         break;
1049 | 
1050 |       case Token.NUMBER:
1051 |         if (undefinedRight) {
1052 |           result = compareToUndefined(left, op);
1053 |           break;
1054 |         }
1055 |         if (nullRight && isEqualityOp(op)) {
1056 |           result = compareToNull(left, op);
1057 |           break;
1058 |         }
1059 |         if (Token.NUMBER != right.getType()) {
1060 |           return n;  // Only eval if they are the same type
1061 |         }
1062 |         Boolean compareResult = compareAsNumbers(op, left, right);
1063 |         if (compareResult != null) {
1064 |           result = compareResult;
1065 |         } else {
1066 |           return null;
1067 |         }
1068 |         break;
1069 | 
1070 |       case Token.NAME:
1071 |         if (leftLiteral && undefinedRight) {
1072 |           result = compareToUndefined(left, op);
1073 |           break;
1074 |         }
1075 | 
1076 |         if (rightLiteral) {
1077 |           boolean undefinedLeft = (left.getString().equals("undefined"));
1078 |           if (undefinedLeft) {
1079 |             result = compareToUndefined(right, op);
1080 |             break;
1081 |           }
1082 |           if (leftLiteral && nullRight && isEqualityOp(op)) {
1083 |             result = compareToNull(left, op);
1084 |             break;
1085 |           }
1086 |         }
1087 | 
1088 |         if (Token.NAME != right.getType()) {
1089 |           return n;  // Only eval if they are the same type
1090 |         }
1091 |         String ln = left.getString();
1092 |         String rn = right.getString();
1093 |         if (!ln.equals(rn)) {
1094 |           return n;  // Not the same value name.
1095 |         }
1096 | 
1097 |         switch (op) {
1098 |           // If we knew the named value wouldn't be NaN, it would be nice
1099 |           // to handle EQ,NE,LE,GE,SHEQ, and SHNE.
1100 |           case Token.LT:
1101 |           case Token.GT:
1102 |             result = false;
1103 |             break;
1104 |           default:
1105 |             return n;  // don't handle that op
1106 |         }
1107 |         break;
1108 | 
1109 |       case Token.NEG:
1110 |         if (leftLiteral) {
1111 |           if (undefinedRight) {
1112 |             result = compareToUndefined(left, op);
1113 |             break;
1114 |           }
1115 |           if (nullRight && isEqualityOp(op)) {
1116 |             result = compareToNull(left, op);
1117 |             break;
1118 |           }
1119 |         }
1120 |         // Nothing else for now.
1121 |         return n;
1122 | 
1123 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1124 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
1125 |       case Token.REGEXP:
1126 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1127 |         if (leftLiteral) {
1128 |           if (undefinedRight) {
1129 |             result = compareToUndefined(left, op);
1130 |             break;
1131 |           }
1132 |           if (nullRight && isEqualityOp(op)) {
1133 |             result = compareToNull(left, op);
1134 |             break;
1135 |           }
1136 |         }
1137 |         // ignore the rest for now.
1138 |         return n;
1139 | 
1140 |       default:
1141 |         // assert, this should cover all consts
1142 |         return n;
1143 |     }
1144 | 
1145 |     Node newNode = NodeUtil.booleanNode(result);
1146 |     n.getParent().replaceChild(n, newNode);
1147 |     reportCodeChange();
1148 | 
1149 |     return newNode;
1150 |   }

3214 |     Node result;

===== M0480 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 409-422
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
409 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
410 |       Node ret;
411 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
412 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
413 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
414 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
415 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
416 |       } else {
417 |         ret = transform(n);
418 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
419 |       }
420 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
421 |       return ret;
422 |     }

===== M0487 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.strEscape(String,char,String,String,String,CharsetEncoder,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1015-1102
Documentation:
/** Helper to escape JavaScript string as well as regular expression */
Method code:
1015 |   private static String strEscape(
1016 |       String s, char quote,
1017 |       String doublequoteEscape,
1018 |       String singlequoteEscape,
1019 |       String backslashEscape,
1020 |       CharsetEncoder outputCharsetEncoder,
1021 |       boolean useSlashV) {
1022 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(s.length() + 2);
1023 |     sb.append(quote);
1024 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1025 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1026 |       switch (c) {
1027 |         case '\0': sb.append("\\x00"); break;
1028 |         case '\u000B':
1029 |           if (useSlashV) {
1030 |             sb.append("\\v");
1031 |           } else {
1032 |             sb.append("\\x0B");
1033 |           }
1034 |           break;
1035 |         // From the SingleEscapeCharacter grammar production.
1036 |         case '\b': sb.append("\\b"); break;
1037 |         case '\f': sb.append("\\f"); break;
1038 |         case '\n': sb.append("\\n"); break;
1039 |         case '\r': sb.append("\\r"); break;
1040 |         case '\t': sb.append("\\t"); break;
1041 |         case '\\': sb.append(backslashEscape); break;
1042 |         case '\"': sb.append(doublequoteEscape); break;
1043 |         case '\'': sb.append(singlequoteEscape); break;
1044 | 
1045 |         // From LineTerminators (ES5 Section 7.3, Table 3)
1046 |         case '\u2028': sb.append("\\u2028"); break;
1047 |         case '\u2029': sb.append("\\u2029"); break;
1048 | 
1049 |         case '>':                       // Break --> into --\> or ]]> into ]]\>
1050 |           if (i >= 2 &&
1051 |               ((s.charAt(i - 1) == '-' && s.charAt(i - 2) == '-') ||
1052 |                (s.charAt(i - 1) == ']' && s.charAt(i - 2) == ']'))) {
1053 |             sb.append("\\>");
1054 |           } else {
1055 |             sb.append(c);
1056 |           }
1057 |           break;
1058 |         case '<':
1059 |           // Break </script into <\/script
1060 |           final String END_SCRIPT = "/script";
1061 | 
1062 |           // Break <!-- into <\!--
1063 |           final String START_COMMENT = "!--";
1064 | 
1065 |           if (s.regionMatches(true, i + 1, END_SCRIPT, 0,
1066 |                               END_SCRIPT.length())) {
1067 |             sb.append("<\\");
1068 |           } else if (s.regionMatches(false, i + 1, START_COMMENT, 0,
1069 |                                      START_COMMENT.length())) {
1070 |             sb.append("<\\");
1071 |           } else {
1072 |             sb.append(c);
1073 |           }
1074 |           break;
1075 |         default:
1076 |           // If we're given an outputCharsetEncoder, then check if the
1077 |           //  character can be represented in this character set.
1078 |           if (outputCharsetEncoder != null) {
1079 |             if (outputCharsetEncoder.canEncode(c)) {
1080 |               sb.append(c);
1081 |             } else {
1082 |               // Unicode-escape the character.
1083 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1084 |             }
1085 |           } else {
1086 |             // No charsetEncoder provided - pass straight Latin characters
1087 |             // through, and escape the rest.  Doing the explicit character
1088 |             // check is measurably faster than using the CharsetEncoder.
1089 |             if (c > 0x1f && c < 0x7f) {
1090 |               sb.append(c);
1091 |             } else {
1092 |               // Other characters can be misinterpreted by some JS parsers,
1093 |               // or perhaps mangled by proxies along the way,
1094 |               // so we play it safe and Unicode escape them.
1095 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1096 |             }
1097 |           }
1098 |       }
1099 |     }
1100 |     sb.append(quote);
1101 |     return sb.toString();
1102 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 274-337
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
274 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
275 |     this.options = options;
276 |     if (errorManager == null) {
277 |       if (outStream == null) {
278 |         setErrorManager(
279 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
280 |       } else {
281 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
282 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
283 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
284 |         setErrorManager(printer);
285 |       }
286 |     }
287 | 
288 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
289 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
290 |       options.checkTypes = true;
291 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
292 |       options.checkTypes = false;
293 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
294 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
295 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
296 |       // parser type warnings.
297 |       options.setWarningLevel(
298 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
299 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
300 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
301 |     }
302 | 
303 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
304 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
305 |       options.setWarningLevel(
306 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
307 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
308 |     }
309 | 
310 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
311 |       options.setWarningLevel(
312 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
313 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
314 |     }
315 | 
316 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
317 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
318 |     guards.add(
319 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
320 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
321 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
322 | 
323 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
324 | 
325 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
326 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
327 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
328 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
329 |     // error off.
330 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
331 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
332 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
333 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
334 |     }
335 | 
336 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
337 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1104-1124
Documentation:

Method code:
1104 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1105 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1106 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1107 |       return s;
1108 |     }
1109 | 
1110 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-Latin characters if needed.
1111 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1112 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1113 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1114 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1115 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1116 |       // crazy.
1117 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1118 |         sb.append(c);
1119 |       } else {
1120 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1121 |       }
1122 |     }
1123 |     return sb.toString();
1124 |   }

Original range: 173-194
Documentation:

Method code:
173 |   public void visit(Node n) {
174 |     Node currentVersionOfNode = n;
175 |     boolean somethingChanged = false;
176 | 
177 |     do {
178 |       somethingChanged = false;
179 |       for (AbstractPeepholeOptimization optimization : peepholeOptimizations) {
180 |         Node newVersionOfNode =
181 |             optimization.optimizeSubtree(currentVersionOfNode);
182 | 
183 |         if (newVersionOfNode != currentVersionOfNode) {
184 |           somethingChanged = true;
185 | 
186 |           currentVersionOfNode = newVersionOfNode;
187 |         }
188 | 
189 |         if (currentVersionOfNode == null) {
190 |           return;
191 |         }
192 |       }
193 |     } while(somethingChanged);
194 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1447-1466
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1447 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1448 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false)) {
1449 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1450 |     }
1451 | 
1452 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1453 |     Node n, n2;
1454 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1455 |          res == null && n != null;
1456 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1457 |       if (node2 == null) {
1458 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1459 |       }
1460 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1461 |       if (res != null) {
1462 |         return res;
1463 |       }
1464 |     }
1465 |     return res;
1466 |   }

===== M0519 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.jsString(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 966-993
Documentation:

Method code:
966 |   private String jsString(String s, boolean useSlashV) {
967 |     int singleq = 0, doubleq = 0;
968 | 
969 |     // could count the quotes and pick the optimal quote character
970 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
971 |       switch (s.charAt(i)) {
972 |         case '"': doubleq++; break;
973 |         case '\'': singleq++; break;
974 |       }
975 |     }
976 | 
977 |     String doublequote, singlequote;
978 |     char quote;
979 |     if (singleq < doubleq) {
980 |       // more double quotes so escape the single quotes
981 |       quote = '\'';
982 |       doublequote = "\"";
983 |       singlequote = "\\\'";
984 |     } else {
985 |       // more single quotes so escape the doubles
986 |       quote = '\"';
987 |       doublequote = "\\\"";
988 |       singlequote = "\'";
989 |     }
990 | 
991 |     return strEscape(s, quote, doublequote, singlequote, "\\\\",
992 |         outputCharsetEncoder, useSlashV);
993 |   }

===== M0550 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryFoldLeftChildOp(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 787-825
Documentation:
/**
   * Expressions such as [foo() * 10 * 20] generate parse trees
   * where no node has two const children ((foo() * 10) * 20), so
   * performArithmeticOp() won't fold it -- tryFoldLeftChildOp() will.
   * Specifically, it folds associative expressions where:
   *  - The left child is also an associative expression of the same time.
   *  - The right child is a constant NUMBER constant.
   *  - The left child's right child is a NUMBER constant.
   */
Method code:
787 |   private Node tryFoldLeftChildOp(Node n, Node left, Node right) {
788 |     int opType = n.getType();
789 |     Preconditions.checkState(
790 |         (NodeUtil.isAssociative(opType) && NodeUtil.isCommutative(opType))
791 |         || n.isAdd());
792 | 
793 |     Preconditions.checkState(
794 |         !n.isAdd()|| !NodeUtil.mayBeString(n));
795 | 
796 |     // Use getNumberValue to handle constants like "NaN" and "Infinity"
797 |     // other values are converted to numbers elsewhere.
798 |     Double rightValObj = NodeUtil.getNumberValue(right);
799 |     if (rightValObj != null && left.getType() == opType) {
800 |       Preconditions.checkState(left.getChildCount() == 2);
801 | 
802 |       Node ll = left.getFirstChild();
803 |       Node lr = ll.getNext();
804 | 
805 |       Node valueToCombine = ll;
806 |       Node replacement = performArithmeticOp(opType, valueToCombine, right);
807 |       if (replacement == null) {
808 |         valueToCombine = lr;
809 |         replacement = performArithmeticOp(opType, valueToCombine, right);
810 |       }
811 |       if (replacement != null) {
812 |         // Remove the child that has been combined
813 |         left.removeChild(valueToCombine);
814 |         // Replace the left op with the remaining child.
815 |         n.replaceChild(left, left.removeFirstChild());
816 |         // New "-Infinity" node need location info explicitly
817 |         // added.
818 |         replacement.copyInformationFromForTree(right);
819 |         n.replaceChild(right, replacement);
820 |         reportCodeChange();
821 |       }
822 |     }
823 | 
824 |     return n;
825 |   }

   * @return A detached clone of the Node and all its children.

1711 |   public Node cloneTree() {

===== M0564 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.endFile()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 513-541
Documentation:

Method code:
513 |     @Override
514 |     void endFile() {
515 |       super.endFile();
516 |       if (!preferLineBreakAtEndOfFile) {
517 |         return;
518 |       }
519 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold / 2) {
520 |         // Add an extra break at end of file.
521 |         append(";");
522 |         startNewLine();
523 |       } else if (prevCutPosition > 0) {
524 |         // Shift the previous break to end of file by replacing it with a
525 |         // <space> and adding a new break at end of file. Adding the space
526 |         // handles cases like instanceof\nfoo. (it would be nice to avoid this)
527 |         code.setCharAt(prevCutPosition, ' ');
528 |         lineStartPosition = prevLineStartPosition;
529 |         lineLength = code.length() - lineStartPosition;
530 |         reportLineCut(lineIndex, prevCutPosition + 1, false);
531 |         lineIndex--;
532 |         prevCutPosition = 0;
533 |         prevLineStartPosition = 0;
534 |         append(";");
535 |         startNewLine();
536 |       } else {
537 |         // A small file with no line breaks. We do nothing in this case to
538 |         // avoid excessive line breaks. It's not ideal if a lot of these pile
539 |         // up, but that is reasonably unlikely.
540 |       }
541 |     }

110 |         validateWhile(n);

121 |       case Token.VAR:
122 |         validateVar(n);
123 |         return;
124 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
125 |         validateExprStmt(n);
126 |         return;
127 |       case Token.RETURN:
128 |         validateReturn(n);
129 |         return;
130 |       case Token.THROW:
131 |         validateThrow(n);
132 |         return;
133 |       case Token.TRY:
134 |         validateTry(n);
135 |         return;
136 |       case Token.BREAK:
137 |         validateBreak(n);
138 |         return;
139 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
140 |         validateContinue(n);
141 |         return;
142 |       case Token.EMPTY:
143 |         validateChildless(n);
144 |         return;
145 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
146 |         validateChildless(n);
147 |         return;
148 |       default:
149 |         violation("Expected statement but was "
150 |             + Token.name(n.getType()) + ".", n);
151 |     }
152 |   }

===== M0575 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 210-238
Documentation:

Method code:
210 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
211 |     maybeEndStatement();
212 | 
213 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
214 |     char prev = getLastChar();
215 | 
216 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
217 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
218 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
221 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
222 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
223 |       append(" ");
224 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
225 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
226 |       append(" ");
227 |     }
228 | 
229 |     // Allow formatting around the operator.
230 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
231 | 
232 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
233 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
234 |     // because it looks strange.
235 |     if (binOp) {
236 |       maybeCutLine();
237 |     }
238 |   }

Method code:
388 |   private void validateVar(Node n) {
389 |     validateNodeType(Token.VAR, n);
390 |     this.validateMinimumChildCount(n, 1);
391 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
392 |       // Don't use the validateName here as the NAME is allowed to have
393 |       // a child.
394 |       validateNodeType(Token.NAME, c);
395 |       validateNonEmptyString(c);
396 |       validateMaximumChildCount(c, 1);
397 |       if (c.hasChildren()) {
398 |         validateExpression(c.getFirstChild());
399 |       }
400 |     }
401 |   }

===== M0584 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1106-1134
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1106 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1107 |     // unknown
1108 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1109 |       return true;
1110 |     }
1111 |     // all type
1112 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1113 |       return true;
1114 |     }
1115 |     // equality
1116 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1117 |       return true;
1118 |     }
1119 |     // unions
1120 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1121 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1122 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1123 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1124 |           return true;
1125 |         }
1126 |       }
1127 |     }
1128 |     // proxy types
1129 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1130 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1131 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1132 |     }
1133 |     return false;
1134 |   }

789 |   private void validateMinimumChildCount(Node n, int i) {
790 |     boolean valid = false;
791 |     if (i == 1) {
792 |       valid = n.hasChildren();
793 |     } else if (i == 2) {
794 |       valid = n.hasMoreThanOneChild();
795 |     } else {
796 |       valid = n.getChildCount() >= i;
797 |     }
798 | 
799 |     if (!valid) {
800 |       violation(
801 |           "Expected at least " + i + " children, but was "
802 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);
803 |     }
804 |   }

===== M0594 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2406-2415
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2406 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2407 |     int len = s.length();
2408 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2409 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2410 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2411 |         return false;
2412 |       }
2413 |     }
2414 |     return true;
2415 |   }

684 |   private static String toSource(Node root, Format outputFormat,

   * Detaches child from Node and replaces it with newChild.
   */
Method code:
716 |   public void replaceChild(Node child, Node newChild) {
717 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.next == null,
718 |         "The new child node has siblings.");
719 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.parent == null,
720 |         "The new child node already has a parent.");
721 | 
722 |     // Copy over important information.
723 |     newChild.copyInformationFrom(child);
724 | 
725 |     newChild.next = child.next;
726 |     newChild.parent = this;
727 |     if (child == first) {
728 |         first = newChild;
729 |     } else {
730 |         Node prev = getChildBefore(child);
731 |         prev.next = newChild;
732 |     }
733 |     if (child == last)
734 |         last = newChild;
735 |     child.next = null;
736 |     child.parent = null;
737 |   }

===== M0608 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 590-627
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is a literal value. We define a literal value
   * as any node that evaluates to the same thing regardless of when or
   * where it is evaluated. So /xyz/ and [3, 5] are literals, but
   * the name a is not.
   *
   * Function literals do not meet this definition, because they
   * lexically capture variables. For example, if you have
   * <code>
   * function() { return a; }
   * </code>
   * If it is evaluated in a different scope, then it
   * captures a different variable. Even if the function did not read
   * any captured variables directly, it would still fail this definition,
   * because it affects the lifecycle of variables in the enclosing scope.
   *
   * However, a function literal with respect to a particular scope is
   * a literal.
   *
   * @param includeFunctions If true, all function expressions will be
   *     treated as literals.
   */
Method code:
590 |   static boolean isLiteralValue(Node n, boolean includeFunctions) {
591 |     switch (n.getType()) {
592 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
593 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
594 |              child = child.getNext()) {
595 |           if ((!child.isEmpty()) && !isLiteralValue(child, includeFunctions)) {
596 |             return false;
597 |           }
598 |         }
599 |         return true;
600 | 
601 |       case Token.REGEXP:
602 |         // Return true only if all children are const.
603 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
604 |              child = child.getNext()) {
605 |           if (!isLiteralValue(child, includeFunctions)) {
606 |             return false;
607 |           }
608 |         }
609 |         return true;
610 | 
611 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
612 |         // Return true only if all values are const.
613 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
614 |              child = child.getNext()) {
615 |           if (!isLiteralValue(child.getFirstChild(), includeFunctions)) {
616 |             return false;
617 |           }
618 |         }
619 |         return true;
620 | 
621 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
622 |         return includeFunctions && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n);
623 | 
624 |       default:
625 |         return isImmutableValue(n);
626 |     }
627 |   }

===== M0609 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getSortedPropTypes()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 881-895
Documentation:
// Gets all the property types, in sorted order.
Method code:
881 |   private int[] getSortedPropTypes() {
882 |     int count = 0;
883 |     for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.getNext()) {
884 |       count++;
885 |     }
886 | 
887 |     int[] keys = new int[count];
888 |     for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.getNext()) {
889 |       count--;
890 |       keys[count] = x.getType();
891 |     }
892 | 
893 |     Arrays.sort(keys);
894 |     return keys;
895 |   }

===== M0613 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.RhinoErrorReporter.replacePlaceHolders(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/RhinoErrorReporter.java
Original range: 65-68
Documentation:
/**
   * For each message such as "Not a good use of {0}", replace the place
   * holder {0} with a wild card that matches all possible strings.
   * Also put the any non-place-holder in quotes for regex matching later.
   */
Method code:
65 |   private Pattern replacePlaceHolders(String s) {
66 |     s = Pattern.quote(s);
67 |     return Pattern.compile(s.replaceAll("\\{\\d+\\}", "\\\\E.*\\\\Q"));
68 |   }
